# Unit Profile Coverage Report

Generated by `scripts/unit-profile-coverage-check.cjs`.

SRD 5.2.1 is conceptually part of Classic, but it is stored separately because the SRD collection has Creative Commons SRD provenance and distribution policy. The Classic 2024 library is a derived view from the SRD collection plus the Classic non-SRD mechanics-only collection; mixed authored provenance is not a valid collection state.

## Metric Semantics

Coverage rows are denominator-specific gates, not weighted completion scores. Unit-scoped denominators count distinct Unit identities; profile-scoped denominators count profile records or per-Unit profile facts as stated. Prose length, deferred-mechanics text, and source-row narrative weight do not add hidden denominator weight.

| Metric | Planning question | Measure | Denominator |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Installed collection inventory count | How many Unit records did the checker discover in the configured installed coverage collections? | installed Unit records discovered from configured collections | n/a; no independent expected-inventory boundary exists |
| Authored Surface Unit catalog admission | How much authored Surface Unit-shaped content is admitted to an installed Unit collection? | authored Surface records whose source path is installed | all authored Surface Unit-shaped records discovered |
| Authored Surface executable catalog admission | How much authored Surface content with executable mechanics is admitted to an installed Unit collection? | authored Surface executable records whose source path is installed | authored Surface Unit-shaped records with executable mechanics |
| Installed Unit profile classification coverage | Does every installed Unit have exactly one supported, unsupported, widening, or assumption disposition? | installed Unit records with a profile disposition claim | installed Unit records discovered from configured collections |
| Supported executable Unit coverage | How much installed executable Unit pressure is mapped to supported mechanics profiles? | installed Units with supported-profile claims | installed Units with executable mechanics |
| QNT profile modeling coverage | Do supported rule profiles that require executable semantics have QNT model owners? | executable profile records with at least one QNT owner | profile records whose kind requires executable evidence |
| QNT proof coverage | Do supported executable profiles have proof evidence? | executable profile records with qnt-proof verification owners | profile records whose kind requires executable evidence |
| Runtime mapping coverage | Do supported executable profiles have production runtime owners? | executable profile records with runtime owners | profile records whose kind requires executable evidence |
| Runtime parity coverage | Do supported executable profiles have focused MBT or runtime-test parity evidence? | executable profile records with focused-mbt or runtime-test verification owners | profile records whose kind requires executable evidence |
| Rules-kernel profile join coverage | Do rules-kernel-admitted mechanics profiles point to rules-kernel semantic obligations? | rules-kernel-applicable profile records with at least one profile-obligation mapping | profile records with rules-kernel profile kinds and either QNT owners or explicit profile-obligation mappings |
| Rules-kernel covered profile coverage | Do rules-kernel-admitted mechanics profiles point to covered rules-kernel obligations? | rules-kernel-applicable profile records whose mapped obligations are all covered | profile records with rules-kernel profile kinds and either QNT owners or explicit profile-obligation mappings |
| Supported Unit rules-kernel chain coverage | Do supported Unit identities have every rules-kernel-admitted profile connected to covered rules-kernel obligations? | supported Unit ids whose rules-kernel-applicable profiles all map to covered obligations | installed Units with supported-profile claims and at least one rules-kernel-applicable profile |
| Deterministic admission/projection coverage | Which supported Unit identities have deterministic production catalog/support/projection evidence? | supported Unit ids with deterministic-admission-projection evidence | installed Units with supported-profile claims |
| Selected identity replay coverage | Which supported Unit identities have intentionally selected concrete identity replay evidence? | supported Unit ids in the replay denominator with selected-identity-mbt evidence | installed Units with supported-profile claims, excluding whole-claim selected-identity not-applicable dispositions |
| Classic non-SRD expression gate | Did every installed Classic non-SRD mechanics-only Unit pass the public-expression gate? | installed Classic non-SRD records passing validation | installed Classic non-SRD records |

## Unit Group Denominator Audit

Background, feat, spell, and class-feature groups are counted from installed Unit identities and supported profile facts. The selected-identity replay denominator is scoped to Unit identities that need replay evidence; whole-claim non-applicable dispositions are excluded, while deferred-portion non-applicable dispositions remain visible in a separate bucket.

| Group | Installed Unit denominator | Executable Unit denominator | Supported-profile Units | Profile-subset Units | Unsupported/other Units | Profile fact denominator | Selected-identity replay witnesses/denominator | Selected-identity replay gaps | Deferred selected-identity non-applicable |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Background Units | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 4 | 0 | 0/0 | 0 | 0 |
| Feat Units | 13 | 13 | 9 | 0 | 4 | 11 | 6/8 | 2 | 0 |
| Spell Units | 136 | 136 | 92 | 18 | 26 | 136 | 110/110 | 0 | 0 |
| Class-feature Units | 93 | 93 | 71 | 8 | 14 | 119 | 74/74 | 0 | 0 |

| Denominator | Rule |
| --- | --- |
| executableUnitDenominator | installed Unit records in the group whose authored mechanics are executable |
| installedUnitDenominator | installed Unit records in the group; each Unit identity counts once |
| profileFactDenominator | sum of profileIds on supported-profile and profile-subset-supported Unit claims; one Unit can carry multiple profile facts |
| selectedIdentityDeferredNonApplicableUnits | profile-subset-supported Unit identities whose deferred mechanics are outside selected-identity replay, while the supported runtime portion still has no selected-identity replay witness |
| selectedIdentityReplayDenominator | supported-profile and profile-subset-supported Unit identities in the group, excluding whole-claim selected-identity not-applicable dispositions; deferred-portion non-applicable rows remain visible in their own bucket |
| selectedIdentityReplayGapUnits | Unit identities in the selected-identity replay denominator with no replay witness and no whole-claim or deferred-portion selected-identity non-applicable disposition |

## Rules-Kernel Join

The Unit matrix owns authored-content breadth. `plans/rules-kernel-coverage/profile-obligations.jsonl` owns the reducer-semantic join from rules-kernel-admitted mechanics profiles to QNT-connected rules-kernel obligations. Projection-only Character Creation and Character Sheet profiles with no QNT owner and no explicit profile-obligation mapping remain covered by their profile runtime evidence rather than this rules-kernel chain.

## Profile Subset Supported Unit Claims

| Unit | Collection | Profiles | Supported Mechanics | Deferred Mechanics |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `species_gnome` | srd-5.2.1 | `character-creation.species-lineage-choice` | Character Creation admits Gnome through the SRD species option set as a fixed Small Humanoid species with 30-foot Speed source facts retained from Surface; Character Creation retains the selected Gnome species Unit ref and authored trait Unit refs for Darkvision, Gnomish Cunning, and Gnomish Lineage without duplicating trait execution state on CharacterBuild; Character Creation consumes the retained Gnomish Lineage trait ref to discover the supported lineage and spellcasting ability choices; Character Creation finalizes selected Gnome plus selected Gnomish Lineage and spellcasting ability as CharacterBuild speciesChoiceFacts | Darkvision sight and illumination projection (closed: outside-runtime-presentation-exploration); Gnomish Cunning Advantage on Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma Saving Throws (closed: outside-battle-runtime); Rock Gnome clockwork-device execution state, table effect choices, activation, duration countdown, concurrent limits, and dismantling (closed: outside-battle-runtime) |
| `druid_wild_shape` | srd-5.2.1 | `character-creation.class-feature-resource-projection`, `character-sheet.class-feature-use-count-resource`, `unit-feature.druid-wild-shape-known-form` | Character Creation admits same-class Druid level-2 progression, retains the Wild Shape feature ref from the Surface class record, and projects the Wild Shape use-count resource into CharacterBuild resources without duplicating class progression state; Character Creation projects Wild Shape character facts from the retained Surface feature, Druid class level, and SRD Stat Block catalog: use-count thresholds, one use regained on Short Rest, all uses regained on Long Rest, rounded-down hour duration, known-form count thresholds, Beast-form eligibility, maximum Challenge Rating thresholds, Fly Speed eligibility, and one eligible known-form replacement on Long Rest; the RAW recommended Stat Blocks remain Surface recommendations rather than default known-form state; Character Sheet requires explicit selected known Beast form Stat Block identities for Wild Shape builds, validates and stores them as current known-form state, tracks expended Wild Shape uses, derives capacity and reset cadence from CharacterBuild resources and Surface feature facts, regains one expended use on Short Rest, clears expended uses on Long Rest, and applies one eligible known-form replacement on Long Rest; App and MCP sheet-creation boundaries accept explicit Wild Shape known-form Stat Block ids or return a typed missing-known-forms workflow issue, and Character Sheet battle handoff preserves current Wild Shape known-form identities when rebuilding a sheet from battle state; Battle runtime promotes a merged-equipment, fallen-equipment dropped-object, not-practical-to-wear fallback, practical worn armor/Shield, practical worn weapon and held-weapon object-use projection, and form-limb object-handling witness, Wild Shape subset: Bonus Action and Wild Shape use spending, caller-selected known Beast form, Druid-level Temporary Hit Points, form Armor Class, Size, Speed, practical worn armor/Shield Armor Class and hand-use projection, practical worn selected-loadout weapon attacks plus held-weapon spell/feature consumers when the active form-limb witness allows object handling, fallen-equipment object-drop outcomes at the shared droppedObjects boundary, active-effect storage of the explicit caller/GM form-limb object-handling witness for object-use consumers, Beast Dexterity Mage Armor AC, supported Stat Block attacks with executable attack-roll damage, including multiple on-hit damage components that preserve component order, damage type, and amount through generic Stat Block damage resolution, form resources, Wild Shape D20 statistic projection for Beast Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution plus retained character Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma, retained Skill and Saving Throw proficiencies using the character Proficiency Bonus, gained Beast proficiencies and higher Beast stat-block modifiers where present, Beast Strength Shove DC while shaped, true-form Unarmed Strike ability modifier preservation, no-spellcasting projection before Beast Spells, Beast Spells level-18 spell invocation admission for existing Spell Invocation owners when the canonical Surface Spell Definition lacks priced or consumed Material components, reversion on dismiss, reuse, Incapacitated, terminal zero Hit Points, or duration expiry, and active-form Character Sheet handoff block; Battle runtime projects active Wild Shape non-D20 creature perception and communication from existing state: Beast-form special Senses and passive Perception come from the selected active form and D20 projection, retained known languages come from CharacterBuild origin and class-feature language facts threaded through character-battle initialization, and retained speech is blocked from condition state without parsing Beast Stat Block language prose or storing duplicate Wild Shape sense/language/speech fields; Battle runtime consumes typed Stat Block trait-derived attack-roll Advantage facts for supported Wild Shape form attacks and applies the Advantage roll mode only from explicit caller spatial/relationship witnesses for the represented non-incapacitated ally within 5 feet of target predicate, without branching on Beast, trait, or Druid authored identity; Battle runtime consumes typed Stat Block attack-hit target Size Prone riders for supported Wild Shape form attacks from parsed Surface on-hit payloads, applies Prone only when the target effective Size is Medium or smaller and not immune to Prone, and keeps non-Prone or otherwise unsupported condition rider shapes closed instead of branching on Beast, attack, or Druid authored identity | Stat Block action shapes outside admitted Wild Shape attacks are split by Surface section/action shape: actions.multiattacks, actions.saves, actions.supports, actionOptions, specials, bonusActions, reactions, and legendaryActions. The current SRD 5.2.1 eligible Beast form catalog has no reachable non-Attack action sections; future records remain closed until the matching generic Stat Block procedure owner has focused evidence. (closed: outside-battle-runtime); Wild Shape generic object-use execution is closed outside the promoted Wild Shape object boundary: generic non-weapon held objects, downstream table placement/use of dropped objects, concrete generic object interaction/Utilize consumers, and any non-resizing/non-reshaping lifecycle beyond selected-loadout object-ref preservation require a generic object/Utilize/table-placement owner. Future consumers must use the stored form-limb witness rather than deriving object handling from Beast authored identity. (closed: outside-battle-runtime); Wild Shape active-form duration across non-battle time remains closed at the Character Sheet handoff boundary by ASSUMPTIONS.md A27 until a session active-effect persistence owner exists. (closed: character-fact-and-runtime-detached-split) |
| `druid_wild_companion` | srd-5.2.1 | `character-creation.class-feature-source-fact-projection` | Wild Companion is authored as a Druid level-2 Surface class-feature record that requires a Magic action, links to the existing Find Familiar Spell Definition, names the SRD spend choices as a Spell Slot or one use of the existing Wild Shape feature resource, removes Material components, fixes the familiar mode to Fey, and adds the caster-finished-Long-Rest disappearance boundary.; Character Creation admits same-class Druid level-2 progression and finalizes retained sibling feature refs for Wild Shape and Wild Companion while Wild Shape's use-count resource is projected by the Druid Wild Shape character-facts owner and Wild Companion is not treated as Wild Shape shape-shifting execution. | Unsupported familiar form attacks and companion command/action selection beyond table choices. (SRDINV86) |
| `monk_monks_focus` | srd-5.2.1 | `character-creation.class-feature-resource-projection`, `character-sheet.class-feature-use-count-resource`, `unit-feature.monk-focus-battle-options` | The Monk class Surface record grants Monk's Focus, Unarmored Movement, and Uncanny Metabolism at Monk level 2, so Character Creation can retain the complete SRD level-2 Monk feature ref set from one class progression source; Surface Monk's Focus represents the shared Focus Point use-count resource with Monk-level linear cap, Short or Long Rest full reset, initial Flurry of Blows, Patient Defense, and Step of the Wind option metadata, the authored Step of the Wind jump-distance multiplier fact, and Wisdom-based class-feature save DC source facts; Character Creation admits same-class Monk level-2 progression and projects the Focus Point resource plus Focus option names and save DC source facts without duplicating class progression or option execution state; Character Sheet tracks expended Focus Point uses, derives the Focus Point cap and Short or Long Rest recovery from CharacterBuild resources and the installed Surface feature, and derives the Monk's Focus save DC from the authored ability source, ability scores, and Proficiency Bonus; Battle runtime admits the Monk's Focus option facts, spends the shared Focus Point resource, executes Flurry of Blows as two granted Unarmed Strike resources, executes Patient Defense free Disengage and Focus-point Disengage plus Dodge modes, executes Step of the Wind free Dash and Focus-point Disengage plus Dash modes, records the paid Step of the Wind turn-scoped jump-distance multiplier, consumes that multiplier through promoted Jump spell movement replacement witnesses, and Character Sheet battle handoff persists shared Focus Point use-count expenditures without adding a synthetic option-local resource pool | Ordinary Long Jump and High Jump movement witnesses are not promoted as standalone runtime commands; their distance derivation, route, obstacle, destination, and landing adjudication remain table-owned movement facts outside this Monk Focus battle profile. (closed: outside-runtime-presentation-exploration) |

The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Distant Spell for promoted range-bearing target owners, currently Touch-range object-light targeting, by deriving a cast-local range fact from the selected Distant Spell and the canonical Surface range shape, spending 1 Sorcery Point from the shared Font of Magic point pool, consuming only range-bearing object target witnesses, preserving the base spell effect such as Light radii, and rejecting unsupported procedures, Self range, sub-5-foot distance ranges, missing range facts, and non-range-bearing fills before resource spending.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Extended Spell for promoted duration-bearing spell owners, currently Enlarge/Reduce creature size change, by deriving a cast-local doubled duration fact from the selected Extended Spell and the canonical Surface duration shape, spending 1 Sorcery Point from the shared Font of Magic point pool, storing the doubled duration on the effect cleanup timer, projecting same-occurrence Concentration maintenance Saving Throw Advantage from the caster's Concentration state, and rejecting unsupported procedures, Instantaneous durations, and sub-1-minute durations before resource spending.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Subtle Spell for action-time spell casts with component requirements by deriving a cast-local component projection from the canonical Surface component shape, spending 1 Sorcery Point from the shared Font of Magic point pool, suppressing Verbal and Somatic components, suppressing focus-replaceable Material components, preserving priced or consumed Material requirements, and rejecting non-action-time or no-component casts before resource spending.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Seeking Spell for missed single spell-attack-damage procedures by reopening the pending Attack Roll fill with a typed Seeking Spell reroll option, enforcing known-option, shared Sorcery Point affordability, spell-attack-only, missed-original-roll, and different-Metamagic stacking gates before spending, applying the forced replacement d20 to hit, miss, natural-1 miss, and natural-20 critical resolution, spending 1 Sorcery Point only when the replacement roll is used, and preserving Ray of Frost's normal damage/effect flow without storing durable reroll opportunity state.; The Metamagic Spell Invocation boundary promotes Empowered Spell for pending single spell-attack-damage rolledDice fills, currently Ray of Frost at level 5, by reopening the damage roll with a typed damage dice reroll option, enforcing known-option, shared Sorcery Point affordability, original-die matching, Charisma-modifier minimum-one selected-die limits, and different-Metamagic stacking gates before spending, applying forced replacement dice before damage totals, spending 1 Sorcery Point only when replacement dice are used, and preserving the base spell damage/effect flow without storing durable reroll opportunity state.; | `gentle_repose` | unsupported-profile | Gentle Repose is authored as an SRD Surface Spell Definition for a consumed-copper-piece Action-or-Ritual touch spell with a 10-day corpse or remains target duration, but Task 30 claims no promoted battle-runtime profile because preventing decay, preventing the target from becoming Undead, and excluding affected days from raising-from-dead time limits are corpse/remains lifecycle and resurrection-deadline facts outside promoted battle Unit profiles. |
| `identify` | unsupported-profile | Magic-item identification, attunement/property discovery, and spell-affecting-object or spell-affecting-creature identification are runtime-detached table adjudication outside promoted battle Unit profiles. |
| `major_image` | unsupported-profile | Major Image is an SRD level-3 Spell Definition whose authored Surface record models Action Spell Slot casting, 120-foot point range, V/S/M fleece component, Concentration up to 10 minutes, a visible point-origin 20-foot Cube illusion, visual/sound/smell/temperature sensory channels, and the no-damage/no-conditions limit. RAW also requires caster Magic Action repositioning and content changes while within range, physical-interaction reveal, Study action Intelligence (Investigation) discovery against Spell Save DC, per-creature see-through and faint sensory qualities after discernment, and level-4+ Spell Slot duration until dispelled without Concentration. No promoted battle-runtime profile is claimed because full support needs an illusion Spell Effect and table/presentation/perception owner for depicted content, sensory believability, image movement and conversation, physical interaction, per-observer discerned state, and duration-kind substitution rather than generic Spell Invocation reducers or duplicated battle map/perception state. |
| `monk_body_and_mind` | unsupported-profile | Body and Mind is a level-20 permanent Dexterity and Wisdom Ability Score increase; no standalone promoted battle Unit profile is claimed for the authored class-feature record. |
| `monk_epic_boon` | unsupported-profile | Level 19 Epic Boon is a later-level feat-selection container; no standalone promoted battle Unit profile is claimed for the authored class-feature record. |
| `paladin_epic_boon` | unsupported-profile | Level 19 Epic Boon is a later-level feat-selection container; no standalone promoted battle Unit profile is claimed for the authored class-feature record. |
| `phantom_steed` | unsupported-profile | Phantom Steed's Surface record has SRD provenance and structured spawned-creature/mount facts for 1-minute-or-Ritual casting, 30-foot point range, V/S components, 1-hour timed duration, an existing Riding Horse catalog reference with a walk Speed 100 override, caster-or-chosen-creature rider permission, saddle/bit/bridle created equipment with a 10-foot vanish boundary, 13 miles/hour table travel, and a one-minute rider dismount grace while the steed fades at spell end. The source prose and closure retain the damage-triggered early spell end because Surface has no created-creature damage subject trigger. No promoted battle-runtime profile is claimed because these clauses require a mount lifecycle/travel owner rather than companion command placeholders. |
| `ranger_feral_senses` | unsupported-profile | Feral Senses is a level-18 Blindsight sense grant; no standalone promoted battle Unit profile is claimed for the authored class-feature record until class-feature sense grants have a sight projection owner. |
| `ranger_tireless` | unsupported-profile | Tireless is a level-10 two-part feature: a Magic action Wisdom-derived Temporary Hit Points use pool plus Short Rest Exhaustion reduction; no standalone promoted battle Unit profile is claimed for the partial authored action shape. |
| `silent_image` | unsupported-profile | Illusory image placement, investigation, physical-interaction reveal, and presentation are runtime-detached illusion/exploration adjudication outside promoted battle Unit profiles. |
| `speak_with_animals` | unsupported-profile | Beast communication, information exchange, and table-mediated social outcomes are runtime-detached exploration/social adjudication outside promoted battle Unit profiles. |
| `tongues` | unsupported-profile | Tongues is an SRD level-3 Spell Definition whose current Surface record models Action casting, Touch range, V/M miniature-ziggurat components, 1-hour duration, one touched creature target, spoken-or-signed language understanding, and outward intelligibility to any creature that knows at least one language and can hear the speech or see the signing. Task 28 claims no promoted battle-runtime profile because hearing or seeing communication, knowing at least one language, language comprehension, and conversation outcomes are runtime-detached table/social communication facts outside promoted battle Unit profiles; the spell must not introduce autonomous behavior, commanded-action execution, or authored-identity dispatch in runtime code. |
| `warlock_contact_patron` | unsupported-profile | Contact Patron is a level-9 always-prepared Contact Other Plane Spell Access and once-per-Long-Rest patron-contact no-slot cast with a feature-scoped automatic Intelligence Saving Throw success; no standalone promoted battle Unit profile is claimed for the authored class-feature record. |
| `water_breathing` | unsupported-profile | Water Breathing is an SRD level-3 Spell Definition whose current Surface record models Action or Ritual casting, 30-foot range, V/S/M short-reed components, 24-hour duration, up to ten willing creature targets, water-breathing grant, and retained normal respiration. Task 29 claims no promoted battle-runtime profile because underwater environment presence, breath-holding timing, suffocation progression, suffocation-caused Exhaustion cleanup when breathing resumes, and any table determination that a creature needs underwater breathing are runtime-detached table/environment hazard facts outside promoted battle Unit profiles. The existing Alter Self Aquatic Adaptation projection is source-specific self-transformation state and is not a generic Water Breathing spell owner. |
